# Target Shortlist

> **Confirm-first workflow:** the scout adds candidate roles here with a **Posted** date. **Nothing gets built or applied to until you set `Pursue? = yes`.** Edit this file (GitHub / Claude app / laptop) to confirm picks. The engine builds ONLY `Pursue? = yes` rows.
> `Posted` = when the role was advertised (best estimate). "Specific posting" = a real advertised role. "Company target" = strong-fit employer (confirm a live opening first).
> Salary filter (from preferences): **≥ AUD 95,000** — drop anything advertised below.

## Remote-first scout — 2026-06-24 (AU / India / Dubai / Saudi / Singapore)

> Triggered by the strategy switch to **remote-first** ahead of a move to India. Ranking weights: **(a) fully remote**, **(b) survives a move to India** (employer hires India-based remote/contract — the highest-value trait), **(c) AI/LLM scope**, **(d) role fit**.
> **"Survives move? = yes"** means the employer hires globally / India-based, so you could keep the role after relocating. **"AU-only"** roles serve the near-term AU-income goal but would NOT follow you to India.
> Salary: AUD 95k floor applies to AU rows only; international rows benchmarked to strong local senior pay (most clear easily).

## How to work this list
- **To apply to something: set its `Pursue? = yes`.** The engine only builds confirmed rows. Unconfirmed rows are just candidates.
- Capital.com + Kogan.com already built (marked `built`).
- The daily scout appends fresh roles (with Posted dates) for you to confirm. It does NOT build them.

## Notes / caveats
- Specific postings were surfaced via aggregators (SEEK, LinkedIn, Built In Melbourne, Wellfound, Fintech Careers). **Confirm each is still live and check work-rights/visa requirements** before applying — this depends on your visa status (see `preferences.md` [CONFIRM]).
- Salary benchmark: Melbourne DE avg ~AUD 141k; experienced up to ~AUD 253k (Glassdoor/SEEK, 2026).
